CX-11 /source_contracts/stale_window

修訂前（原包保留）
"Confirm acquires trusted input read-fences in order definition→approval→design BEFORE active writer lease, after rendering/preview. Fences authenticate captured revisions and remain held through staging/seal/active CAS receipt; producer mutations return Busy/defer while held, never merely invalidate a token after final check. No lock upgrade or producer callback while writer held; release all on every terminal result. This may temporarily block definition/design edits and is an explicit Owner lifecycle choice. If native/provider cannot express held cross-process revision fences, DependencyNotReady; no check-then-write safety claim."

CX-04/CX-11 /source_contracts/design_port

修訂前（原包保留）
"DesignSource.capture_saved(context) -> DesignEvidence {project/document/session/generation,revision,format,semantic_profile,digest,canonical_payload,formal_save_proof}; revalidate(evidence) under owner fence. Initial source proposal requires current captured revision exactly covered by verified M04 formal Saved proof (including pending M05 transfer-before-ACK amendment if adopted); dirty/deferred/missing proof returns DesignSaveRequired. User chooses explicit save separately. Headless/blank UI generation may omit design only when definition explicitly authorizes empty design contract; never infer approval from absent doc."

修訂後候選：

"Capture saved M04 content/profile plus authenticated formal FileCommit +M05 root TransferReceipt +ACK provenance; dirty/deferred proof returns DesignSaveRequired, never save implicitly. Definition/approval/design source reservations held before active writer through receipt."

CX-12 /store_contract/private_IO_seam

修訂前（原包保留）
"Generation read lease must inspect current IO journal under shared OS lock compatible with active FileCommit writer. Proposed bounded crate-private file_commit.rs helper exposes ONLY readonly terminal/active consistency validation for already-locked canonical active target. No journal format rewrite, cleanup, public Port extension or bypass. This is new IO-owned integration scope requiring approval/review, not implied by accepted IO product. If wrapper cannot preserve IO invariants, REPLAN_REQUIRED before implementation."

修訂後候選：

"IO-owned crate-private CommittedReadGuard unforgeable outside adapter constructor. inspect_committed_locked(&CommittedReadGuard)->Result<CommittedReadInspection,CommitError>; validates guard-bound canonical target/terminal journal/current raw digest, no path argument replacement. Public FileCommitPort unchanged."
